**Step 4:** The spatial reasoning used here involves understanding the sequence of events and the physical state of the victim at the time of death. The Autopsy Report (Evidence 11) indicates that the chest wounds were inflicted after death, suggesting a specific sequence of events that does not align with the implication of Testimony 3 that the victim was able to retaliate after being murdered.

**Step 5:** Given the analysis, the contradiction lies between the information provided in Evidence 11 (Autopsy Report) and Testimony 3 (Gravelle's testimony about the victim's retaliation after being murdered).

**Step 3:** **If applicable propositions exist,** explicitly state which proposition(s) and explain how they reveal the contradiction

**Your answer must end with a JSON format like so:** {"evidence": 2, "testimony": 3}

**The answer JSON should strictly be the last line, followed by nothing else.**

LLM OUTPUT (RAW RESPONSE):
------------------------------
**Step 1:** Analyzing the spatial contradiction between evidences and testimonies, we notice that Kay's recollections (Evidence 8) mention a person coming from beyond the concession stand and pushing her off the building. However, Testimony 3 by Kay mentions seeing something red from under the cherry tree.

**Step 2:** Checking if any spatial propositions apply to this contradiction, we find that Proposition 2 states: "If the concession stand is in front of the cherry tree and Kay is behind the concession stand then Kay is in front of the cherry tree." This proposition is relevant because it describes the spatial relationship between the concession stand, Kay, and the cherry tree.

**Step 3:** Applying Proposition 2, if Kay was behind the concession stand (as implied by her recollection of someone coming from beyond the concession stand), then she would be in front of the cherry tree, not under it. This contradicts Testimony 3, where Kay feels like she saw something red from under the cherry tree.

**Step 4:** The spatial reasoning used here involves understanding the layout of the viewing platform as described in the Bigg Building Pamphlet (Evidence 7), which places the cherry tree at the left-most edge and the candy stand (concession stand) at the right-most edge. Given this layout, being behind the concession stand would indeed place Kay in front of the cherry tree, not under it.

**Step 5:** Based on the analysis, the contradiction is between Evidence 8 (Kay's Recollections) and Testimony 3 (Kay's testimony about seeing something red from under the cherry tree).

{"evidence": 8, "testimony": 3}

{
  "evidence": 8,
  "testimony": 3
